import torch
import tilelang
import tilelang.language as T


_KERNEL_CACHE = {}
_WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY = None
_WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE = None
_WEIGHT_CACHE = {}
_EXPERT_BLOCK_M = 192


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _pack_experts_kernel(
    hidden,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    packed_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, hidden)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        stacked_expert_tokens: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        packed_input: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    packed_input[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        stacked_expert_tokens[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j]
                    )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _unpack_experts_kernel(
    hidden,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
    expert_start,
    expert_chunk,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    packed_shape = (expert_chunk, expert_block_m, hidden)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        packed_output: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
        routed_expert_weights: T.Tensor((total_valid_tokens,), T.float32),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        out: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            expert_chunk,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (local_expert_id, bn):
            expert_id = expert_start + local_expert_id
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            raw_start = group_offsets[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    out[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        packed_output[local_expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                        * routed_expert_weights[raw_start + i]
                    )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _swiglu_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        up_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        activation: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    gate = gate_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                    activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        gate
                        * T.sigmoid(gate)
                        * up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                    )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
):
    block_i = 64
    block_h = 256
    threads = 256

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        up_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        combined_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate * 2, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate * 2, block_i),
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_h),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bi, bh):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_i, block_h):
                row = bi * block_i + i
                col = bh * block_h + j
                if row < intermediate:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = gate_w[expert_id, row, col]
                else:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = up_w[
                        expert_id, row - intermediate, col
                    ]

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _combined_swiglu_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    combined_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate * 2)
    activation_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_up_output: T.Tensor(combined_shape, T.float16),
        activation: T.Tensor(activation_shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                gate = gate_up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                    gate
                    * T.sigmoid(gate)
                    * gate_up_output[
                        expert_id,
                        i,
                        intermediate + bn * block_n + j,
                    ]
                )

    return kernel


def _get_kernels(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
):
    key = (
        int(hidden),
        int(intermediate),
        int(num_experts),
        int(total_padded_tokens),
        int(total_valid_tokens),
        int(expert_block_m),
    )
    kernels = _KERNEL_CACHE.get(key)
    if kernels is None:
        expert_chunk = 32 if num_experts == 32 else 16
        fuse_fc1 = False
        kernels = (
            _pack_experts_kernel(
                hidden,
                num_experts,
                total_padded_tokens,
                expert_block_m,
            ),
            _swiglu_kernel(
                intermediate,
                num_experts,
                expert_block_m,
            ),
            (
                _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(hidden, intermediate, num_experts)
                if fuse_fc1
                else None
            ),
            (
                _combined_swiglu_kernel(
                    intermediate,
                    num_experts,

import torch
import tilelang
import tilelang.language as T


_KERNEL_CACHE = {}
_WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY = None
_WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE = None
_WEIGHT_CACHE = {}
_EXPERT_BLOCK_M = 192


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _pack_experts_kernel(
    hidden,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    packed_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, hidden)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        stacked_expert_tokens: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        packed_input: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    packed_input[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        stacked_expert_tokens[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j]
                    )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _unpack_experts_kernel(
    hidden,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
    expert_start,
    expert_chunk,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    packed_shape = (expert_chunk, expert_block_m, hidden)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        packed_output: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
        routed_expert_weights: T.Tensor((total_valid_tokens,), T.float32),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        out: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            expert_chunk,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (local_expert_id, bn):
            expert_id = expert_start + local_expert_id
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            raw_start = group_offsets[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    out[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        packed_output[local_expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                        * routed_expert_weights[raw_start + i]
                    )

    return kernel



@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }

def _unpack_split_experts_kernel(
    hidden,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    split_m,
    row_base,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        split_output: T.Tensor((num_experts, split_m, hidden), T.float16),
        routed_expert_weights: T.Tensor((total_valid_tokens,), T.float32),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        out: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            raw_start = group_offsets[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(split_m, block_n):
                global_i = row_base + i
                if global_i < group_size:
                    out[padded_start + global_i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        split_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                        * routed_expert_weights[raw_start + global_i]
                    )

    return kernel

@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _swiglu_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        up_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        activation: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            group_size = group_offsets[expert_id + 1] - group_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    gate = gate_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                    activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        gate
                        * T.sigmoid(gate)
                        * up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                    )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
):
    block_i = 64
    block_h = 256
    threads = 256

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        up_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        combined_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate * 2, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate * 2, block_i),
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_h),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bi, bh):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_i, block_h):
                row = bi * block_i + i
                col = bh * block_h + j
                if row < intermediate:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = gate_w[expert_id, row, col]
                else:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = up_w[
                        expert_id, row - intermediate, col
                    ]

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _combined_swiglu_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    combined_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate * 2)
    activation_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_up_output: T.Tensor(combined_shape, T.float16),
        activation: T.Tensor(activation_shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                gate = gate_up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                    gate
                    * T.sigmoid(gate)
                    * gate_up_output[
                        expert_id,
                        i,
                        intermediate + bn * block_n + j,
                    ]
                )

    def kernel(

@tilelang.jit(

def _swiglu_prescaled_kernel(intermediate, num_experts, total_valid_tokens, expert_block_m):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        up_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        routed_expert_weights: T.Tensor((total_valid_tokens,), T.float32),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        activation: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(num_experts, T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n), threads=threads) as (expert_id, bn):
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            raw_start = group_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                gate = gate_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                if i < group_size:
                    activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        gate * T.sigmoid(gate)
                        * up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                        * routed_expert_weights[raw_start + i]
                    )
                else:
                    activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = T.float16(0)

    return kernel

    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)

    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        up_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        activation: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            group_size = group_offsets[expert_id + 1] - group_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    gate = gate_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                    activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        gate
                        * T.sigmoid(gate)
                        * up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                    )

    return kernel

    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
):
    block_i = 64
    block_h = 256
    threads = 256

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        up_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        combined_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate * 2, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate * 2, block_i),
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_h),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bi, bh):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_i, block_h):
                row = bi * block_i + i
                col = bh * block_h + j
                if row < intermediate:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = gate_w[expert_id, row, col]
                else:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = up_w[
                        expert_id, row - intermediate, col
                    ]

    return kernel

    hidden,

    total_padded_tokens,
    expert_block_m,

    threads = 256
    packed_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, hidden)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        stacked_expert_tokens: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        packed_input: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
    ):

            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):

            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):

                    packed_input[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        stacked_expert_tokens[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j]
                    )

    return kernel

def _unpack_experts_kernel(
    hidden,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
    expert_start,
    expert_chunk,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    packed_shape = (expert_chunk, expert_block_m, hidden)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        packed_output: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
        routed_expert_weights: T.Tensor((total_valid_tokens,), T.float32),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        out: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            expert_chunk,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (local_expert_id, bn):
            expert_id = expert_start + local_expert_id
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            raw_start = group_offsets[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    out[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        packed_output[local_expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                        * routed_expert_weights[raw_start + i]
                    )

    return kernel

def _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(

    intermediate,
    num_experts,

    block_i = 64
    block_h = 256
    threads = 256

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        up_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        combined_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate * 2, hidden), T.float16),

@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _combined_swiglu_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    combined_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate * 2)
    activation_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_up_output: T.Tensor(combined_shape, T.float16),
        activation: T.Tensor(activation_shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                gate = gate_up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                    gate
                    * T.sigmoid(gate)
                    * gate_up_output[
                        expert_id,
                        i,
                        intermediate + bn * block_n + j,
                    ]
                )

    return kernel


def _get_kernels(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
):
    key = (
        int(hidden),
        int(intermediate),
        int(num_experts),
        int(total_padded_tokens),
        int(total_valid_tokens),
        int(expert_block_m),
    )
    kernels = _KERNEL_CACHE.get(key)
    if kernels is None:
        expert_chunk = 32 if num_experts == 32 else (24 if num_experts == 64 else 16)
        fuse_fc1 = False
        kernels = (
            _pack_experts_kernel(
                hidden,
                num_experts,
                total_padded_tokens,
                expert_block_m,
            ),
            _swiglu_kernel(
                intermediate,
                num_experts,
                expert_block_m,
            ),
            (
                _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(hidden, intermediate, num_experts)
                if fuse_fc1
                else None
            ),
            (
                _combined_swiglu_kernel(
                    intermediate,
                    num_experts,

def _bucket_compact_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
    bucket_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        activation: T.Tensor(
            (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate), T.float16
        ),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        bucket_activation: T.Tensor(
            (num_experts, bucket_m, intermediate), T.float16
        ),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(bucket_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    bucket_activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                    )
                else:
                    bucket_activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = T.float16(0)

    return kernel

                if i < group_size:
                    gate = gate_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                    activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        gate
                        * T.sigmoid(gate)
                        * up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                    )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
):
    block_i = 64
    block_h = 256
    threads = 256

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        up_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        combined_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate * 2, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate * 2, block_i),
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_h),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bi, bh):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_i, block_h):
                row = bi * block_i + i
                col = bh * block_h + j
                if row < intermediate:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = gate_w[expert_id, row, col]
                else:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = up_w[
                        expert_id, row - intermediate, col
                    ]

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _combined_swiglu_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    combined_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate * 2)
    activation_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_up_output: T.Tensor(combined_shape, T.float16),
        activation: T.Tensor(activation_shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                gate = gate_up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                    gate
                    * T.sigmoid(gate)
                    * gate_up_output[
                        expert_id,
                        i,
                        intermediate + bn * block_n + j,
                    ]
                )

    return kernel


def _get_kernels(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
):
    key = (
        int(hidden),
        int(intermediate),
        int(num_experts),
        int(total_padded_tokens),
        int(total_valid_tokens),
        int(expert_block_m),
    )
    kernels = _KERNEL_CACHE.get(key)
    if kernels is None:
        expert_chunk = 32 if num_experts == 32 else 16
        fuse_fc1 = False
        kernels = (
            _pack_experts_kernel(
                hidden,
                num_experts,
                total_padded_tokens,
                expert_block_m,

                intermediate,
                num_experts,
                expert_block_m,
            ),
            (
                _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(hidden, intermediate, num_experts)
                if fuse_fc1
                else None
            ),
            (
                _combined_swiglu_kernel(
                    intermediate,
                    num_experts,

     return kernel

+@tilelang.jit(
+    pass_configs={
+        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
+        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,

+def _bucket_compact_kernel(
+    intermediate,
+    num_experts,
+    expert_block_m,
+    bucket_m,
+):
+    block_n = 256
+    threads = 256
+
+    @T.prim_func
+    def kernel(
+        activation: T.Tensor(
+            (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate), T.float16
+        ),
+        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
+        bucket_activation: T.Tensor(
+            (num_experts, bucket_m, intermediate), T.float16
+        ),
+    ):
+        with T.Kernel(
+            num_experts,
+            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
+            threads=threads,
+        ) as (expert_id, bn):
+            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
+            for i, j in T.Parallel(bucket_m, block_n):
+                if i < group_size:
+                    bucket_activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
+                        activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
+                    )
+                else:
+                    bucket_activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = T.float16(0)
+
+    return kernel
+
+
+@tilelang.jit(
+    pass_configs={
+        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
+        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
+    }
+)
+def _bucket_unpack_kernel(
+    hidden,
+    num_experts,
+    total_padded_tokens,
+    total_valid_tokens,
+    bucket_m,
+):
+    block_n = 256
+    threads = 256
+
+    @T.prim_func
+    def kernel(
+        bucket_output: T.Tensor((num_experts, bucket_m, hidden), T.float16),
+        routed_expert_weights: T.Tensor((total_valid_tokens,), T.float32),
+        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
+        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
+        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
+        out: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
+    ):
+        with T.Kernel(
+            num_experts,
+            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
+            threads=threads,
+        ) as (expert_id, bn):
+            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
+            raw_start = group_offsets[expert_id]
+            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
+            for i, j in T.Parallel(bucket_m, block_n):
+                if i < group_size:
+                    out[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j] = (
+                        bucket_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
+                        * routed_expert_weights[raw_start + i]
+                    )
+
+    return kernel
+

+def _unpack_experts_copy_kernel(hidden, num_experts, total_padded_tokens, expert_block_m, expert_start, expert_chunk):
+    block_n = 256
+    threads = 256
+    packed_shape = (expert_chunk, expert_block_m, hidden)
+
+    @T.prim_func
+    def kernel(
+        packed_output: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
+        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
+        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
+        out: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
+    ):
+        with T.Kernel(expert_chunk, T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n), threads=threads) as (local_expert_id, bn):
+            expert_id = expert_start + local_expert_id
+            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
+            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
+            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
+                if i < group_size:
+                    out[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j] = packed_output[local_expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
+
+    return kernel
+

+        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
+    }

+def _swiglu_prescaled_kernel(intermediate, num_experts, total_valid_tokens, expert_block_m):
+    block_n = 256
+    threads = 256
+    shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)
+
+    @T.prim_func
+    def kernel(
+        gate_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
+        up_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
+        routed_expert_weights: T.Tensor((total_valid_tokens,), T.float32),
+        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
+        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
+        activation: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
+    ):
+        with T.Kernel(num_experts, T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n), threads=threads) as (expert_id, bn):
+            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
+            raw_start = group_offsets[expert_id]
+            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
+                gate = gate_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
+                if i < group_size:
+                    activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
+                        gate * T.sigmoid(gate)
+                        * up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
+                        * routed_expert_weights[raw_start + i]
+                    )
+                else:
+                    activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = T.float16(0)
+
+    return kernel
+

+        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
+        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
+    }

+def _unpack_split_experts_kernel(
+    hidden,
+    num_experts,
+    total_padded_tokens,
+    total_valid_tokens,
+    split_m,
+    row_base,
+):
+    block_n = 256
+    threads = 256
+
+    @T.prim_func
+    def kernel(
+        split_output: T.Tensor((num_experts, split_m, hidden), T.float16),
+        routed_expert_weights: T.Tensor((total_valid_tokens,), T.float32),
+        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
+        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
+        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
+        out: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
+    ):
+        with T.Kernel(
+            num_experts,
+            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
+            threads=threads,
+        ) as (expert_id, bn):
+            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
+            raw_start = group_offsets[expert_id]
+            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
+            for i, j in T.Parallel(split_m, block_n):
+                global_i = row_base + i
+                if global_i < group_size:
+                    out[padded_start + global_i, bn * block_n + j] = (
+                        split_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
+                        * routed_expert_weights[raw_start + global_i]
+                    )
+
+    return kernel

ROUNDING

def _get_workspace(
    stacked_expert_tokens,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    global _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY, _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE
    hidden = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[1])
    key = (
        int(stacked_expert_tokens.device.index or 0),
        int(num_experts),
        hidden,
        int(intermediate),
        int(expert_block_m),
        str(stacked_expert_tokens.dtype),
    )
    if _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY != key:
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY = None
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE = None
        packed_input = torch.empty(
            (num_experts, expert_block_m, hidden),
            device=stacked_expert_tokens.device,
            dtype=stacked_expert_tokens.dtype,
        )
        activation = (
            torch.empty(
                (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate),
                device=stacked_expert_tokens.device,
                dtype=stacked_expert_tokens.dtype,
            )
            if False
            else None
        )
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY = key
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE = (packed_input, activation)
    return _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE


def _get_combined_fc1_weight(gate_w, up_w, combine_weights):
    key = (
        int(gate_w.device.index or 0),
        int(gate_w.shape[0]),
        int(gate_w.shape[1]),
        int(gate_w.shape[2]),
        str(gate_w.dtype),
    )
    cached = _WEIGHT_CACHE.get(key)
    if cached is None or cached[0] is not gate_w or cached[1] is not up_w:
        combined_w = torch.empty(
            (gate_w.shape[0], gate_w.shape[1] * 2, gate_w.shape[2]),
            device=gate_w.device,
            dtype=gate_w.dtype,
        )
        combine_weights(gate_w, up_w, combined_w)
        cached = (gate_w, up_w, combined_w)
        _WEIGHT_CACHE[key] = cached
    return cached[2]


def run_kernel(
    stacked_expert_tokens,
    gate_w,
    up_w,
    down_w,
    routed_expert_weights,
    group_sizes,
    group_offsets,
    group_padded_offsets,
    group_idx_for_bx,
    out,
):
    hidden = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[1])
    intermediate = int(gate_w.shape[1])
    num_experts = int(gate_w.shape[0])
    total_padded_tokens = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[0])
    total_valid_tokens = int(routed_expert_weights.shape[0])

    expert_block_m = _EXPERT_BLOCK_M

    pack, swiglu, combine_weights, combined_swiglu, unpack_chunks = _get_kernels(
        hidden,
        intermediate,
        num_experts,
        total_padded_tokens,
        total_valid_tokens,
        expert_block_m,
    )
    packed_input, activation = _get_workspace(
        stacked_expert_tokens,
        intermediate,
        num_experts,
        expert_block_m,
    )

    pack(
        stacked_expert_tokens,
        group_sizes,
        group_padded_offsets,
        packed_input,
    )
    gate_output = packed_input @ gate_w.transpose(1, 2)
    up_output = packed_input @ up_w.transpose(1, 2)
    swiglu(gate_output, up_output, group_offsets, gate_output)
    del up_output
    activation = gate_output
    expert_chunk = 32 if num_experts == 32 else 16
    for chunk_id, expert_start in enumerate(range(0, num_experts, expert_chunk)):
        expert_end = min(expert_start + expert_chunk, num_experts)
        packed_output = (
            activation[expert_start:expert_end]
            @ down_w[expert_start:expert_end].transpose(1, 2)
        )
        unpack_chunks[chunk_id](
            packed_output,
            routed_expert_weights,
            group_sizes,
            group_offsets,
            group_padded_offsets,
            out,
        )
        del packed_output

def _get_workspace(
    stacked_expert_tokens,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    global _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY, _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE
    hidden = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[1])
    key = (
        int(stacked_expert_tokens.device.index or 0),
        int(num_experts),
        hidden,
        int(intermediate),
        int(expert_block_m),
        str(stacked_expert_tokens.dtype),
    )
    if _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY != key:
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY = None
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE = None
        packed_input = torch.empty(
            (num_experts, expert_block_m, hidden),
            device=stacked_expert_tokens.device,
            dtype=stacked_expert_tokens.dtype,
        )
        activation = (
            torch.empty(
                (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate),
                device=stacked_expert_tokens.device,
                dtype=stacked_expert_tokens.dtype,
            )
            if False
            else None
        )
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY = key
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE = (packed_input, activation)
    return _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE


def _get_combined_fc1_weight(gate_w, up_w, combine_weights):
    key = (
        int(gate_w.device.index or 0),
        int(gate_w.shape[0]),
        int(gate_w.shape[1]),
        int(gate_w.shape[2]),
        str(gate_w.dtype),
    )
    cached = _WEIGHT_CACHE.get(key)
    if cached is None or cached[0] is not gate_w or cached[1] is not up_w:
        combined_w = torch.empty(
            (gate_w.shape[0], gate_w.shape[1] * 2, gate_w.shape[2]),
            device=gate_w.device,
            dtype=gate_w.dtype,
        )
        combine_weights(gate_w, up_w, combined_w)
        cached = (gate_w, up_w, combined_w)
        _WEIGHT_CACHE[key] = cached
    return cached[2]


def run_kernel(
    stacked_expert_tokens,
    gate_w,
    up_w,
    down_w,
    routed_expert_weights,
    group_sizes,
    group_offsets,
    group_padded_offsets,
    group_idx_for_bx,
    out,
):
    hidden = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[1])
    intermediate = int(gate_w.shape[1])
    num_experts = int(gate_w.shape[0])
    total_padded_tokens = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[0])
    total_valid_tokens = int(routed_expert_weights.shape[0])

    expert_block_m = _EXPERT_BLOCK_M

    pack, swiglu, combine_weights, combined_swiglu, unpack_chunks = _get_kernels(
        hidden,
        intermediate,
        num_experts,
        total_padded_tokens,
        total_valid_tokens,
        expert_block_m,
    )
    packed_input, activation = _get_workspace(
        stacked_expert_tokens,
        intermediate,
        num_experts,
        expert_block_m,
    )

    pack(
        stacked_expert_tokens,
        group_sizes,
        group_padded_offsets,
        packed_input,
    )
    gate_output = packed_input @ gate_w.transpose(1, 2)
    up_output = packed_input @ up_w.transpose(1, 2)
    swiglu(gate_output, up_output, group_sizes, gate_output)
    del up_output
    activation = gate_output
    expert_chunk = 32 if num_experts == 32 else 16
    for chunk_id, expert_start in enumerate(range(0, num_experts, expert_chunk)):
        expert_end = min(expert_start + expert_chunk, num_experts)
        packed_output = (
            activation[expert_start:expert_end]
            @ down_w[expert_start:expert_end].transpose(1, 2)
        )
        unpack_chunks[chunk_id](
            packed_output,
            routed_expert_weights,
            group_sizes,
            group_offsets,
            group_padded_offsets,
            out,
        )
        del packed_output

 succeeded in 1182ms:

### v142_exact_row_fc2.py

- Parent: v141.
- Architectural change: replace chunked padded FC2 BMM plus custom unpack with per-expert exact-row high-level matmul, routed-weight multiply, and direct output copy.
- Codex indexing/lifetime gate and C500 numerical validation passed all official shapes.
- XPUOJ submission 105811: Accepted, score 80.33, at 2.324 / 4.667 / 8.784 ms (sample case1 2.314).
- Versus v141: +14.8% / +26.3% / +33.8%, score -3.34.
- Root cause: Python `.item()` synchronization, E host-controlled iterations, and many small matmul/copy launches dominate increasingly with workload size.
- Conclusion: rollback and block the exact-row per-expert host-sync/many-launch family. Next architecture must vectorize metadata and use batched/grouped device execution.

### v144_e64_split128_64_fc2.py

- Parent: v141.
- E64-only architecture: replace four expert-chunk16 FC2 BMMs with two all-expert row-split BMMs at M128 and M64, followed by split unpack kernels.
- Codex implementation and C500 correctness gates passed all shapes.
- XPUOJ submission 105834 unexpectedly WrongAnswer on unchanged E16; Codex allowed one unchanged retry because the E16 path was byte-equivalent.
- Retry submission 105839: Accepted, score 82.33, at 2.046 / 3.721 / 8.559 ms.
- Versus v141: +1.09% / +0.70% / +30.40%; score -1.34. The accepted run also emitted a TileLang static data-race warning in split unpack.
- Conclusion: rollback and block all-expert row-split BMM. Do not continue row-split enumeration; future grouped execution must use vectorized device metadata and avoid warning-prone scalar indexing.

### v145_e64_prescale_copy_unpack.py

- Parent: v141.
- E64-only shallow dataflow change: multiply routed weights into guarded SwiGLU activation, retain standard chunk16 FC2, and replace weighted unpack with copy-only unpack. E16/E32 remain parent paths.
- Codex implementation and C500 numerical gates passed; E64 max error increased modestly to 0.00439453125 but remained within official tolerance.
- XPUOJ submission 105862: Accepted, score 83.67, at 2.030 / 3.692 / 6.562 ms.
- Versus v141: +0.006 / -0.003 / -0.001 ms, entirely within noise.
- Conclusion: archive as accepted-neutral, do not promote. Combined with the earlier E32 result, mark routed-weight prescaling saturated and change direction.

### v146_e16_up_slab_stream_fc1.py

- Parent: v141.
- E16-only architecture: retain full gate BMM but split up BMM into two N4096 slabs, applying guarded SwiGLU after each slab to halve peak `up_output` memory.
- Codex implementation and C500 correctness gates passed all official shapes.
- XPUOJ submission 105913: Accepted, score 83.67, at 2.121 / 3.668 / 6.572 ms (sample case1 2.123).
- Changed E16 regressed 0.097 ms (+4.79%); E32/E64 movement is noise. Aggregate time regressed 0.64%.
- Conclusion: rollback and block FC1 output-slab streaming. Lower peak memory does not offset extra BMM/SwiGLU launches and worse slab GEMM efficiency.

### v147_e32_static_m176.py

- Parent: v141.
- Evidence correction and experiment: v114 dynamic M176 had been blocked by forbidden `.item()` rather than a numerical failure, so v147 tried a host-sync-free static E32 `expert_block_m=176` based on the C500 harness observing max group size170.
- Codex implementation and C500 all-shape numerical gates passed.
- XPUOJ submission 106723 failed on unchanged E16, so Codex allowed one unchanged retry.
- Retry submission 106729: E16/E64 Accepted at 2.043/6.634 ms, but changed E32 WrongAnswer with a missing valid output (`target=0`, `ref=3.408203125`).
- Conclusion: the OJ E32 routing distribution is not bounded by the local harness's observed max170; static M176 truncates valid rows. Roll back and block all static M<192 specialization unless the official contract provides a hard bound.

### v143_e32_bucketed_fc2.py

- Parent: v141.
- Proposed architecture: E32-only fixed `M=128` compact activation, one standard batched FC2, and direct bucket unpack with device metadata and no host scalar reads.
- Codex pre-C500 gate blocked the implementation: official case2 has expert counts from 119 to 170, so `M=128` deterministically drops valid rows 128-169. Safe fixed M is192 and gives no compute reduction; true bucketing requires device-side multi-bucket routing/grouped GEMM.
- Conclusion: no C500/OJ submission. Do not use fixed M128 for E32; retain the vectorized grouped-execution goal but redesign the bucket mechanism.

### v140_case1_fc1_swiglu_tilelang.py

- Parent: v136.

- Corrected submission 105721: WrongAnswer at output `[1506,58]`, error 0.01397. A suspected parallel scalar/store race was removed by serializing the row loop.
- Race-fixed submission 105747: WrongAnswer at `[2127,109]`, error 0.12256, and catastrophic case1 user time 54.572 ms versus 11.275 ms baseline.
- Codex final conclusion: OJ failure pattern remains unstable despite repeated C500 passes, and performance is structurally unacceptable. Abandon this custom dense FC1+SwiGLU TileLang family rather than continue patching it.
- Direction switch: retain standard high-level GEMM-backed FC1 and investigate only safe shallow fusion/dataflow changes around it, or pivot to a different architectural family.

import torch
import tilelang
import tilelang.language as T


_KERNEL_CACHE = {}
_WORKSPACE_CACHE = {}
_WEIGHT_CACHE = {}
_EXPERT_BLOCK_M = 192


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _pack_experts_kernel(
    hidden,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    packed_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, hidden)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        stacked_expert_tokens: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        packed_input: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    packed_input[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        stacked_expert_tokens[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j]
                    )
                else:
                    packed_input[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = T.float16(0)

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _unpack_experts_kernel(
    hidden,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
    expert_start,
    expert_chunk,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    packed_shape = (expert_chunk, expert_block_m, hidden)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        packed_output: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
        routed_expert_weights: T.Tensor((total_valid_tokens,), T.float32),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        out: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            expert_chunk,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (local_expert_id, bn):
            expert_id = expert_start + local_expert_id
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            raw_start = group_offsets[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    out[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        packed_output[local_expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                        * routed_expert_weights[raw_start + i]
                    )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _swiglu_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        up_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        activation: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                gate = gate_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                    gate
                    * T.sigmoid(gate)
                    * up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
):
    block_i = 64
    block_h = 256
    threads = 256

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        up_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        combined_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate * 2, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate * 2, block_i),
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_h),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bi, bh):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_i, block_h):
                row = bi * block_i + i
                col = bh * block_h + j
                if row < intermediate:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = gate_w[expert_id, row, col]
                else:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = up_w[
                        expert_id, row - intermediate, col
                    ]

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _combined_swiglu_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    combined_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate * 2)
    activation_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_up_output: T.Tensor(combined_shape, T.float16),
        activation: T.Tensor(activation_shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                gate = gate_up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                    gate
                    * T.sigmoid(gate)
                    * gate_up_output[
                        expert_id,
                        i,
                        intermediate + bn * block_n + j,
                    ]
                )

    return kernel


def _get_kernels(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
):
    key = (
        int(hidden),
        int(intermediate),
        int(num_experts),
        int(total_padded_tokens),
        int(total_valid_tokens),
        int(expert_block_m),
    )
    kernels = _KERNEL_CACHE.get(key)
    if kernels is None:
        expert_chunk = 64 if num_experts == 32 else 16
        fuse_fc1 = False
        kernels = (
            _pack_experts_kernel(
                hidden,
                num_experts,
                total_padded_tokens,
                expert_block_m,
            ),
            _swiglu_kernel(
                intermediate,
                num_experts,
                expert_block_m,
            ),
            (
                _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(hidden, intermediate, num_experts)
                if fuse_fc1
                else None
            ),
            (
                _combined_swiglu_kernel(
                    intermediate,
                    num_experts,
                    expert_block_m,
                )

codex
**FAMILY_RANKING**

1. **E64 four-output single unpack launch**: keep E64 FC2 chunk16 BMMs exactly as v149, but defer unpack and launch one TileLang unpack kernel over all four chunk outputs. Real launch-count change: E64 unpack launches `4 -> 1`. Selected.
2. **Runtime-generic per-chunk unpack kernel**: replace four compiled unpack specializations with one compiled kernel taking `expert_start`. This reduces kernel-cache tuple specialization, but still launches four times, so gain is weak.
3. **Standard-op FC2 reshaping/fusion**: full-batch FC2, row-split FC2, exact-row FC2, prescale/copy-unpack. Rejected as duplicate/blocked by v142/v144/v145/v148 family evidence.

**DUPLICATE_AUDIT**

Not duplicate of v137/v138: chunk size remains E64 chunk16, no chunk20/24 enumeration.

Not duplicate of v144: no all-expert row-split BMM, no M128/M64 split, no split unpack warning-prone scalar row mapping.

Not duplicate of v145: routed-weight multiply remains in unpack, no prescale arithmetic change.

Not duplicate of v148/full-batch FC2: no single `(64,192,2048) @ (64,2048,7168)` output path.

v149 metadata substitution remains intact but is not extended.

**SELECTED_ARCHITECTURE**

E64-only combined unpack kernel.

Current E64 path:

```python
for chunk in 0..3:
    packed_output = activation[chunk*16:(chunk+1)*16] @ down_w[...].transpose(1, 2)
    unpack_chunks[chunk](packed_output, ...)
    del packed_output
```

v150 E64 path:

```python
po0 = activation[0:16] @ down_w[0:16].transpose(1, 2)
po1 = activation[16:32] @ down_w[16:32].transpose(1, 2)
po2 = activation[32:48] @ down_w[32:48].transpose(1, 2)
po3 = activation[48:64] @ down_w[48:64].transpose(1, 2)

unpack_e64_four(po0, po1, po2, po3, routed_expert_weights,
                group_sizes, group_offsets, group_padded_offsets, out)

del po0, po1, po2, po3
```

E16/E32 use the exact v149 loop and unpack tuple unchanged.

**NUMBERED_MODIFICATION_POINTS**

1. Add `_unpack_e64_four_chunks_kernel(hidden, total_padded_tokens, total_valid_tokens, expert_block_m)`.
2. In `_get_kernels`, return this kernel only when `num_experts == 64`.
3. Keep the existing `unpack_chunks` tuple for E16/E32.
4. In `run_kernel`, branch only after `activation = gate_output`:
   `if num_experts == 64: four BMMs + one unpack_e64_four`, else current loop.
5. Do not change pack, FC1 BMMs, SwiGLU, routed-weight math, M192, chunk16/32/16 policy, caches, or metadata.

**GRID_MAPPING_PROOF**

Kernel grid:

```python
T.Kernel(4, 16, T.ceildiv(hidden, 256), threads=256)
```

Indices:

```python
chunk_id        in [0, 3]
local_expert_id in [0, 15]
bn              in [0, ceildiv(hidden, 256)-1]

expert_id = chunk_id * 16 + local_expert_id
col = bn * 256 + j
```

Input tensor selected by `chunk_id`:

```python
0 -> po0[local_expert_id, i, col] maps expert 0..15
1 -> po1[local_expert_id, i, col] maps expert 16..31
2 -> po2[local_expert_id, i, col] maps expert 32..47
3 -> po3[local_expert_id, i, col] maps expert 48..63
```

Output mapping remains:

```python
raw_start = group_offsets[expert_id]
padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
if i < group_sizes[expert_id]:
    out[padded_start + i, col] = packed_value * routed_expert_weights[raw_start + i]
```

**RACE_PROOF**

For different `expert_id`, `group_padded_offsets[expert_id]` defines disjoint packed row intervals. Within one expert, each `(bn, j)` maps to one hidden column, so no two blocks write the same `(row, col)`. `chunk_id` only partitions experts; it never duplicates an expert. Therefore all writes are disjoint.

**MEMORY_LIFETIME**

No extra retained cross-case memory. `_WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY/_VALUE` unchanged. `_WEIGHT_CACHE` unchanged.

The cost is transient E64 peak memory: four FC2 chunk outputs stay live until the combined unpack instead of one-at-a-time. They are local variables and must be deleted immediately after the single unpack. This is the main tradeoff.

**EXPECTED_GAIN**

Small but real target: remove three E64 TileLang unpack launches and three Python unpack dispatches. Expected case3 gain is likely `0.01-0.05 ms`; score may remain `83.67`. No expected E16/E32 movement except judge noise.

**CORRECTNESS_RISKS**

Main compile risk is TileLang lowering of four tensor inputs with dynamic `chunk_id` selection. Use uniform per-block `if chunk_id == k` branches.

Numerical risk is minimal: same BMM outputs, same multiply placement, same fp16 store, same valid-row guard.

**PERFORMANCE_RISKS**

Holding four outputs live may increase allocator pressure and erase launch savings. Single larger unpack launch may also schedule differently than four smaller launches. If case3 regresses by more than noise, roll back.

**IMPLEMENTATION_INSTRUCTIONS**

Implement from `v149_swiglu_group_sizes_metadata.py` as `v150_e64_four_unpack_launch.py`.

Keep E16/E32 source byte-equivalent in `run_kernel` except tuple destructuring needed to receive the optional E64 kernel. Add only the E64 branch. Run `rg "\.item\("` and diff against v149 before C500.

**C500_GATE**

Pass all three official shapes. Check max error, zero padding, immutable inputs. Block if TileLang reports a static data-race warning or if E16/E32 differ numerically from v149.

**XPUOJ_GATE**

Submit only after C500 pass. Accept only if status is Accepted. Promotion requires case3 not worse than v149 `6.567 ms` beyond noise; any WrongAnswer or case3 regression over roughly `0.05 ms` rolls back.

**FINAL_GATE**

PASS_TO_IMPLEMENT.
tokens used
132,243
**FAMILY_RANKING**

1. **E64 four-output single unpack launch**: keep E64 FC2 chunk16 BMMs exactly as v149, but defer unpack and launch one TileLang unpack kernel over all four chunk outputs. Real launch-count change: E64 unpack launches `4 -> 1`. Selected.
2. **Runtime-generic per-chunk unpack kernel**: replace four compiled unpack specializations with one compiled kernel taking `expert_start`. This reduces kernel-cache tuple specialization, but still launches four times, so gain is weak.
3. **Standard-op FC2 reshaping/fusion**: full-batch FC2, row-split FC2, exact-row FC2, prescale/copy-unpack. Rejected as duplicate/blocked by v142/v144/v145/v148 family evidence.

**DUPLICATE_AUDIT**

Not duplicate of v137/v138: chunk size remains E64 chunk16, no chunk20/24 enumeration.

Not duplicate of v144: no all-expert row-split BMM, no M128/M64 split, no split unpack warning-prone scalar row mapping.

Not duplicate of v145: routed-weight multiply remains in unpack, no prescale arithmetic change.

Not duplicate of v148/full-batch FC2: no single `(64,192,2048) @ (64,2048,7168)` output path.

v149 metadata substitution remains intact but is not extended.

**SELECTED_ARCHITECTURE**

E64-only combined unpack kernel.

Current E64 path:

```python
for chunk in 0..3:
    packed_output = activation[chunk*16:(chunk+1)*16] @ down_w[...].transpose(1, 2)
    unpack_chunks[chunk](packed_output, ...)
    del packed_output
```

v150 E64 path:

```python
po0 = activation[0:16] @ down_w[0:16].transpose(1, 2)
po1 = activation[16:32] @ down_w[16:32].transpose(1, 2)
po2 = activation[32:48] @ down_w[32:48].transpose(1, 2)
po3 = activation[48:64] @ down_w[48:64].transpose(1, 2)

unpack_e64_four(po0, po1, po2, po3, routed_expert_weights,
                group_sizes, group_offsets, group_padded_offsets, out)

del po0, po1, po2, po3
```

E16/E32 use the exact v149 loop and unpack tuple unchanged.

**NUMBERED_MODIFICATION_POINTS**

1. Add `_unpack_e64_four_chunks_kernel(hidden, total_padded_tokens, total_valid_tokens, expert_block_m)`.
2. In `_get_kernels`, return this kernel only when `num_experts == 64`.
3. Keep the existing `unpack_chunks` tuple for E16/E32.
4. In `run_kernel`, branch only after `activation = gate_output`:
   `if num_experts == 64: four BMMs + one unpack_e64_four`, else current loop.
5. Do not change pack, FC1 BMMs, SwiGLU, routed-weight math, M192, chunk16/32/16 policy, caches, or metadata.
